n[0B101000] = (function()
    local D; D = (nil)
    local t, B, R, C, X, w, Z
    w, C, X, B, R, Z = E:MQ(B, Z, w, R, X, n, C)

    local U, _, G, h
    _, G, h, U = E:NQ(h, n, G, U, B, _)

    local a
    a, Z, G, D, _, h, t =
        E:jC(U, n, G, a, _, X, R, w, B, h, Z, C)

    if D == -0b10 then
        return t
    elseif D == -0B1 then
        return
    elseif D then
        return E.J(D)
    end

    D, t = E:nC(a, C, n, B)
    if D == -1 then
        return
    elseif D == -2 then
        return t
    end
end)

k = (function()
    local D, t, B
    D, B, t = E:NC(n, B)
    if D == -0X1 then
        return
    elseif D == -2 then
        return t
    elseif D then
        return E.J(D)
    end

    D = E:SC(n, B)
    if D == -0X1 then
        return
    elseif D then
        return E.J(D)
    end
end)

I = function(...)
    return (...)()
end

P = nil
p = (23)

-- ===============================
-- ⬇️ TÁCH DC() RA Ở ĐÂY
-- ===============================
local __OBJ__ = (function()
    return P, I, k, p
end)()

-- ===============================
-- 🔥 HOOK DISPATCHER (LOGIC DUMP)
-- ===============================
local __VM_DUMP__ = {}
local __oldJ = E.J

E.J = function(op, ...)
    __VM_DUMP__[#__VM_DUMP__ + 1] = tostring(op)
    return __oldJ(op, ...)
end

-- auto copy sau khi VM chạy
task.spawn(function()
    task.wait(2)
    if #__VM_DUMP__ > 0 then
        setclipboard(table.concat(__VM_DUMP__, "\n"))
        warn("[VM] dumped", #__VM_DUMP__, "ops")
    else
        warn("[VM] no opcode captured")
    end
end)
